{"result":"{\"success\": true, \"data\": {\"stdout\": \"\\\"\\\"\\\"Prepare SciERC SFT training data for W5 LoRA fine-tuning.\\n\\nPurpose\\n-------\\nConvert SciERC sentence-level annotations into a prompt-completion JSONL\\nthat is **byte-identical** to what `run_constrained_extraction.py` feeds\\nthe model at inference time. This guarantees that the LoRA-tuned model\\ncan be evaluated by the existing eval script with zero code changes.\\n\\nInputs\\n------\\n- SciERC JSONL (default: /root/schema_semantic_constrained_ie/data/processed_data/train.json)\\n- Qwen3-8B tokenizer (for chat_template + enable_thinking=False rendering)\\n\\nOutputs\\n-------\\n- JSONL with one line per sentence, fields:\\n    {\\\"prompt\\\": \\\"&lt;chat-templated text up to assistant generation start>\\\",\\n     \\\"completion\\\": \\\"&lt;gold JSON string>\\\",\\n     \\\"doc_id\\\": \\\"...\\\", \\\"n_entities\\\": int, \\\"n_relations\\\": int}\\n- TRL SFTTrainer auto-detects prompt-completion format and (by default)\\n  computes loss only on the completion tokens.\\n\\nKey details / why this is non-trivial\\n-------------------------------------\\n* The eval script calls `tokenizer.apply_chat_template(..., enable_thinking=False)`.\\n  For Qwen3 this still injects an empty `&lt;think>\\\\n\\\\n&lt;/think>\\\\n\\\\n` block before\\n  the assistant response — we must render the prompt with the same flag, so the\\n  fine-tuned model learns to emit JSON immediately after that block (not after\\n  a real `&lt;think>` section).\\n* SCIERC system/user templates are copied verbatim from\\n  `run_constrained_extraction.py` (SCIERC_SYSTEM / SCIERC_USER_TEMPLATE).\\n  Any drift here breaks the train/eval prompt match.\\n* Sentences with zero gold entities are skipped — training on\\n  `{\\\"entities\\\": [], \\\"relations\\\": []}` would push the model toward empty output.\\n\\\"\\\"\\\"\\nimport argparse\\nimport json\\nimport os\\nimport sys\\nfrom pathlib import Path\\n\\nfrom transformers import AutoTokenizer\\n\\n\\n# Copied verbatim from run_constrained_extraction.py — DO NOT EDIT independently.\\nSCIERC_SYSTEM = \\\"\\\"\\\"You are an expert information extraction system for scientific texts. Extract entities and relations from the given text.\\n\\nEntity types: Task, Method, Material, Metric, OtherScientificTerm, Generic\\nRelation types: Used-for, Feature-of, Hyponym-of, Part-of, Compare, Conjunction, Evaluate-for\\n\\nOutput a JSON object with:\\n- \\\"entities\\\": list of {\\\"text\\\": \\\"...\\\", \\\"type\\\": \\\"...\\\"}\\n- \\\"relations\\\": list of {\\\"head\\\": \\\"...\\\", \\\"tail\\\": \\\"...\\\", \\\"type\\\": \\\"...\\\", \\\"head_type\\\": \\\"...\\\", \\\"tail_type\\\": \\\"...\\\"}\\n\\nOutput ONLY valid JSON, no explanation.\\\"\\\"\\\"\\n\\nSCIERC_USER_TEMPLATE = \\\"\\\"\\\"Extract all entities and relations from this scientific text:\\n\\n\\\"{text}\\\"\\n\\nOutput JSON:\\\"\\\"\\\"\\n\\nRAW_TO_CANONICAL = {\\n    \\\"USED-FOR\\\": \\\"Used-for\\\", \\\"FEATURE-OF\\\": \\\"Feature-of\\\",\\n    \\\"HYPONYM-OF\\\": \\\"Hyponym-of\\\", \\\"PART-OF\\\": \\\"Part-of\\\",\\n    \\\"COMPARE\\\": \\\"Compare\\\", \\\"CONJUNCTION\\\": \\\"Conjunction\\\",\\n    \\\"EVALUATE-FOR\\\": \\\"Evaluate-for\\\",\\n}\\n\\n\\ndef load_scierc_samples(data_path):\\n    \\\"\\\"\\\"Reproduce load_scierc_data() from run_constrained_extraction.py.\\\"\\\"\\\"\\n    samples = []\\n    with open(data_path) as f:\\n        for line in f:\\n            line = line.strip()\\n            if not line:\\n                continue\\n            doc = json.loads(line)\\n            doc_id = doc.get(\\\"doc_key\\\", \\\"unknown\\\")\\n            sentences = doc.get(\\\"sentences\\\", [])\\n            ner_all = doc.get(\\\"ner\\\", [])\\n            rel_all = doc.get(\\\"relations\\\", [])\\n\\n            offsets, cum = [], 0\\n            for s in sentences:\\n                offsets.append(cum)\\n                cum += len(s)\\n\\n            for sent_idx, tokens in enumerate(sentences):\\n                sentence_text = \\\" \\\".join(tokens)\\n                offset = offsets[sent_idx]\\n\\n                gold_entities = []\\n                if sent_idx < len(ner_all):\\n                    for start, end, etype in ner_all[sent_idx]:\\n                        mention = \\\" \\\".join(tokens[start - offset:end - offset + 1])\\n                        gold_entities.append({\\\"text\\\": mention, \\\"type\\\": etype})\\n\\n                gold_relations = []\\n                if sent_idx < len(rel_all):\\n                    span_type = {}\\n                    if sent_idx < len(ner_all):\\n           \\n\\n... [截断: 输出过长，省略 9 字符] ...\\n\\n    for s, e, et in ner_all[sent_idx]:\\n                            span_type[(s, e)] = et\\n                    for h_s, h_e, t_s, t_e, rtype_raw in rel_all[sent_idx]:\\n                        head_text = \\\" \\\".join(tokens[h_s - offset:h_e - offset + 1])\\n                        tail_text = \\\" \\\".join(tokens[t_s - offset:t_e - offset + 1])\\n                        head_type = span_type.get((h_s, h_e), \\\"UNKNOWN\\\")\\n                        tail_type = span_type.get((t_s, t_e), \\\"UNKNOWN\\\")\\n                        rtype = RAW_TO_CANONICAL.get(rtype_raw, rtype_raw)\\n                        gold_relations.append({\\n                            \\\"head\\\": head_text, \\\"tail\\\": tail_text, \\\"type\\\": rtype,\\n                            \\\"head_type\\\": head_type, \\\"tail_type\\\": tail_type,\\n                        })\\n\\n                samples.append({\\n                    \\\"doc_id\\\": f\\\"{doc_id}_sent{sent_idx}\\\",\\n                    \\\"input_text\\\": sentence_text,\\n                    \\\"gold_entities\\\": gold_entities,\\n                    \\\"gold_relations\\\": gold_relations,\\n                })\\n    return samples\\n\\n\\ndef build_prompt(tokenizer, sample):\\n    \\\"\\\"\\\"Render chat template identically to run_constrained_extraction.py.\\\"\\\"\\\"\\n    messages = [\\n        {\\\"role\\\": \\\"system\\\", \\\"content\\\": SCIERC_SYSTEM},\\n        {\\\"role\\\": \\\"user\\\", \\\"content\\\": SCIERC_USER_TEMPLATE.format(text=sample[\\\"input_text\\\"])},\\n    ]\\n    # enable_thinking=False matches eval-time behaviour for Qwen3.\\n    return tokenizer.apply_chat_template(\\n        messages, tokenize=False, add_generation_prompt=True, enable_thinking=False,\\n    )\\n\\n\\ndef build_completion(sample):\\n    \\\"\\\"\\\"Gold JSON the model must reproduce. Compact (no spaces) matches greedy decode shape.\\\"\\\"\\\"\\n    obj = {\\\"entities\\\": sample[\\\"gold_entities\\\"], \\\"relations\\\": sample[\\\"gold_relations\\\"]}\\n    return json.dumps(obj, ensure_ascii=False)\\n\\n\\ndef main():\\n    p = argparse.ArgumentParser(description=\\\"Convert SciERC to SFT JSONL for W5 LoRA training.\\\")\\n    p.add_argument(\\\"--input\\\", p.add_argument(\\\"--keep_empty\\\", dest=\\\"skip_empty\\\", action=\\\"store_false\\\")\\n    args = p.parse_args()\\n\\n    print(f\\\"Loading tokenizer from {args.model_path}\\\", flush=True)\\n       "result": "{\"success\": true, \"data\": {\"stdout\": \"\\\"\\\"\\\"Phase 2: Constrained extraction with grammar (XGrammar) + semantic (TypeConstraintProcessor) layers.\\n\\nModes:\\n  unconstrained  - no constraints (baseline)\\n  grammar_only   - XGrammar JSON/enum structure enforcement\\n  semantic_only  - TypeConstraintProcessor domain/range masking\\n  full_s1        - both layers, single-pass\\n  full_s2        - two-pass: entities first, then constrained relations\\n\\nDatasets: scierc (sentence-level), docred (document-level)\\nOutput: JSONL compatible with violation_checker.py\\n\\\"\\\"\\\"\\n\\nimport argparse\\nimport json\\nimport os\\nimport re\\nimport time\\nfrom pathlib import Path\\n\\nimport torch\\nfrom transformers import AutoModelForCausalLM, AutoTokenizer\\n\\nfrom compiler.ontology_compiler import ConstraintSpec, RelationConstraint\\nfrom compiler.type_mask_processor import TypeConstraintProcessor\\n\\n\\ndef _chat_template_kwargs(tokenizer):\\n    name = getattr(tokenizer, 'name_or_path', '').lower()\\n    if 'qwen' in name:\\n        return {'enable_thinking': False}\\n    return {}\\n\\n\\nRAW_TO_CANONICAL = {\\n    \\\"USED-FOR\\\": \\\"Used-for\\\", \\\"FEATURE-OF\\\": \\\"Feature-of\\\",\\n    \\\"HYPONYM-OF\\\": \\\"Hyponym-of\\\", \\\"PART-OF\\\": \\\"Part-of\\\",\\n    \\\"COMPARE\\\": \\\"Compare\\\", \\\"CONJUNCTION\\\": \\\"Conjunction\\\",\\n    \\\"EVALUATE-FOR\\\": \\\"Evaluate-for\\\",\\n}\\n\\n# DocRED entity type order must match Phase 0 (run_extraction_docred.py)\\nDOCRED_ENTITY_TYPES = [\\\"PER\\\", \\\"ORG\\\", \\\"LOC\\\", \\\"TIME\\\", \\\"NUM\\\", \\\"MISC\\\"]\\n\\nSCIERC_SYSTEM = \\\"\\\"\\\"You are an expert information extraction system for scientific texts. offset:end - offset + 1])\\n                        gold_entities.append({\\\"text\\\": mention, \\\"type\\\": etype})\\n\\n                gold_relations = []\\n                if sent_idx < len(rel_all):\\n                    span_type = {}\\n                    if sent_idx < len(ner_all):\\n                        for s, e, et in ner_all[sent_idx]:\\n                            span_type[(s, e)] = et\\n                    for h_s, h_e, t_s, t_e, rtype_raw in rel_all[sent_idx]:\\n                        head_text = \\\" \\\".join(tokens[h_s - relation type, and treats those as the\\nlegitimate constraint set.\\n\\\"\\\"\\\"\\n\\nimport json\\nimport argparse\\nfrom collections import defaultdict, Counter\\nfrom pathlib import Path\\n\\n\\ndef load_data(train_path: str, rel_info_path: str):\\n    with open(train_path) as f:\\n        train = json.load(f)\\n    with open(rel_info_path) as f:\\n        rel_info = mention).\\\"\\\"\\\"\\n    type_counts = Counter(m.get(\\\"type\\\", \\\"UNK\\\") for m in vertex_set_entry)\\n    return type_counts.most_common(1)[0][0]\\n\\n\\ndef analyze_constraints(train: valid_pair_counts.append(len(valid_pairs))\\n\\n    unseen = [r for r in rel_info if r not in relation_constraints]\\n\\n    result = {\\n        \\\"entity_types\\\": sorted(entity_types_seen),\\n        \\\"relation_types\\\": rel_info,\\n        \\\"relation_constraints\\\": relation_constraints,\\n        \\\"summary\\\": {\\n            \\\"total_triples\\\": total_triples,\\n            \\\"total_relation_types_in_data\\\": len(relation_constraints),\\n            \\\"total_relation_types_defined\\\": len(rel_info),\\n            \\\"unseen_relation_types\\\": unseen,\\n            \\\"avg_valid_pairs_per_relation\\\": round(\\n                sum(valid_pair_counts) / len(valid_pair_counts), 2\\n            )\\n\", \"stderr\": \"\", \"return_code\": 0}, \"error\": null, sorted(relation_pairs.items()):\\n        rel_name = rel_info.get(rel_id, rel_id)\\n        valid_pairs = sorted(set(pair_counter.keys()))\\n        pair_dist = {f\\\"{h}->{t}\\\": c for (h, t), c in pair_counter.most_common()}\\n        relation_constraints[rel_id] = {\\n            \\\"name\\\": rel_name,\\n            \\\"valid_pairs\\\": [list(p) for p in valid_pairs],\\n            \\\"stats\\\": {\\n                \\\"total\\\": sum(pair_counter.values()),\\n                \\\"pair_distribution\\\": pair_dist,\\n            },\\n        }\\n        valid_pair_counts.append(len(valid_pairs))\\n\\n    unseen = [r for r in rel_info if r not in relation_constraints]\\n\\n    result = {\\n        \\\"entity_types\\\": sorted(entity_types_seen),\\n        \\\"relation_types\\\": rel_info,\\n        \\\"relation_constraints\\\": relation_constraints,\\n        \\\"summary\\\": {\\n            \\\"total_triples\\\": total_triples,\\n            \\\"total_relation_types_in_data\\\": len(relation_constraints),\\n            \\\"total_relation_types_defined\\\": len(rel_info),\\n            \\\"unseen_relation_types\\\": unseen,\\n            \\\"avg_valid_pairs_per_relation\\\": round(\\n                sum(valid_pair_counts) / len(valid_pair_counts), 2\\n            )\\n\", \"stderr\": \"\", \"return_code\": 0}, \"error\": null, \"metadata\": {\"elapsed_seconds\": 0.45}}"
    }
  }
}

# 可行性报告

## Part 1: cuda:1 状态 — **不干净**
```
PID    283829, mem=17442 MiB, util=85%, etime=29:39
CMD:   python3 run_constrained_extraction.py --mode full_s1 --dataset docred
       --data_path data/docred/dev.json --device cuda:0 --batch_size 1 --max_new_tokens 4096
```
进程已运行 30 分钟，**不是刚杀掉的残留**——很可能是 W1 DocRED CF-fallback 实验仍在 cuda:1 上跑（命令里写 `--device cuda:0` 但实际显示在 GPU 1，说明启动时设了 `CUDA_VISIBLE_DEVICES=1`）。需 Director 确认：是要保留这个 inference job，还是杀掉？

## Part 2: DocRED SFT 数据生成可行性

### SciERC SFT 格式（已有参考实现）
- 脚本：`scripts/prepare_sft_data_scierc.py`（230 行，结构清晰）
- 训练样本：1762 条 sentence-level（350 docs × ~5 sents，过滤 0-entity 后）
- 格式：`{prompt, completion, doc_id, n_entities, n_relations}` JSONL
- prompt = Qwen3 chat template（system+user, `enable_thinking=False`，含空 `&lt;think>` block）
- completion = compact gold JSON
- **关键设计**：prompt/completion 模板从 `run_constrained_extraction.py` 完全复用（SCIERC_SYSTEM/SCIERC_USER_TEMPLATE），保证 train/eval byte-identical

### DocRED 数据现状
- `train_annotated.json`: 13 MB ≈ 3053 docs（标准 DocRED）
- `dev.json`: 4.3 MB ≈ 1000 docs
- `ontology_constraints_docred.json`: 已有，包含 96 relations + 6 entity types (PER/ORG/LOC/TIME/NUM/MISC) + 训练数据导出的 valid_pairs
- `rel_info.json`: 96 个 P-id → 关系名映射
- `run_constrained_extraction.py:120-160` 已有 `load_docred_data()` 实现 + DocRED prompt builder（document-level, 不分句）

### 适配工作量
**新脚本 `prepare_sft_data_docred.py` 需要做的事**：
1. 复用 `load_docred_data()` 逻辑（直接 import 或拷贝）
2. 复用 `build_messages("docred", ...)` 拼 prompt（已存在）
3. 加载 `ontology_constraints_docred.json` 取 `relation_types` 传给 build_messages
4. 同样的 chat template rendering + compact JSON completion 序列化
5. 同样的 skip-empty 过滤逻辑

**核心 80% 代码可直接复用 SciERC 版本，只换 loader + relation_types 来源。**

### Prompt/Completion 长度预估
| 部分 | 字符 | tokens |
|---|---|---|
| Boilerplate (entity types, schema 提示) | ~500 | ~150 |
| 96 relations list (`- {name}\n`) | ~2400 | ~600 |
| Document text (~200 tokens, doc-level) | ~1000 | ~250 |
| **Prompt total** | | **~1000-1200** |
| Completion: 19 entities + 12 relations gold JSON | ~2800 | ~700-900 |
| **Sample total** | | **~1700-2100** |

### 关键风险
1. **`max_length=2048` 需要上调至 ≥3072（建议 4096）**——SciERC 的 2048 不够 DocRED
2. **训练显存**：seq_len 4096 vs SciERC 的 ~512，per-token 计算量 ~8x。bs=4 grad_accum=4 可能 OOM，建议 bs=2 / grad_accum=8 起步，或先 dry-run 测一下
3. **训练时长**：3053 docs × 3 epochs / (bs=2×ga=8=eff_bs=16) ≈ 572 steps。SciERC 是 1762/16×3≈330 steps 用了 688s。DocRED 序列更长（4x），估计 **40-60 分钟 / single GPU**
4. **数据量充足，无需采样**——3053 docs 比 SciERC 350 大很多，应该能学得更好
5. **96 relations 长 prompt 会放大 violation 的影响**——这正是 composability 评估关心的点，符合 W5 目的

### 预估工作量
- 写 `prepare_sft_data_docred.py`: **0.5h**（复用度高）
- 数据生成运行: **2-3 min**（CPU-bound, tokenizer pass）
- 适配 `train_lora_docred.py`（建议新建而非改 SciERC 版）: **0.5h**（改 data_files 路径 + max_length + 可能调 batch）
- LoRA 训练运行: **40-60 min** 单卡
- merge + 评估: **~10 min**

**总计：~1.5h 开发 + ~1h GPU 时间**

### 建议
**直接适配，无需新方案。** 现有 SciERC pipeline 设计良好，只需要替换数据 loader 和扩大 max_length。建议步骤：
1. 先等 Director 决定 cuda:1 那个 DocRED 进程的去留
2. 起 `prepare_sft_data_docred.py`（基于 SciERC 版改）→ 生成 jsonl
3. dry_run 验证 prompt 渲染正确（特别是 96-relation list 顺序稳定性）
4. 起 `train_lora_docred.py`，先 `--dry_run` 测显存峰值，再正式 3 epochs
5. 评估同 SciERC 流程：unconstrained → full_s1，看 violation 下降
